- **qa-explorer**: Use this agent to QA-test a single Medika frontend page like a real user trying to break it — running the negative/validation checks from docs/qa/check-catalog.md, capturing screenshots + console + network evidence, and returning STRUCTURED findings (it does not file issues; the /qa-sweep skill dedups and files). Invoke one per page. &lt;example&gt; Context: The orchestrator wants the profile page checked. user: "QA the /profile page on local." assistant: "I'll launch the qa-explorer agent against /profile and collect structured findings." &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: A nightly sweep fans out one… ([akqira/Medika](https://github.com/akqira/Medika/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-explorer.md))
- **qa-explorer**: Read-only exploratory-QA agent for the protospy UI.
